Potential Capital Deployment Opportunities Report

Every stock meeting the signed rules' Early Stage Indicator, Late Stage Indicator, trigger day or healthy confirmation check on the 27-Nov-2026 close, by script from the daily classification file. Call-outs go to your Capital Deployment Priority stocks and to a stock in one of your six emphasis groups at the Early Stage Indicator or later (Late Stage Indicator, trigger day, confirmation day); every other stock is in the tables. Look at the charts on the Investment Classification pages of the Master Dashboard, the Multi-stock Dashboard and the Per-stock Dashboard (a name opens it). Ranked and described, never a recommendation to trade.

Position Entry and Exit Rules, version of 13-Nov-2026 with Richard's rulings to 19-Nov-2026; built 28-Nov-2026 13:31 by scripts/capital_deployment_reports.py 1.2-2026-11-27.
